**Quotation convention.** Every fenced block below is a byte-exact substring of one of the four sources above, lifted programmatically:

- control-event quotations are the **exact raw line** of `control_events.jsonl` for that `sequence`, unmodified (the file stores one compact JSON object per line);
- `verdict.json` quotations are exact substrings located by span-scan of the compact file (no re-serialisation);
- context quotations are the exact JSON-encoded value of `history[i].<field>` from `contexts/phase_*.json` (still JSON-escaped, exactly as the file stores it), so `\n` appears as the two characters backslash-n;
- `full_outputs.jsonl` quotations are the exact JSON-encoded `output` value for that `ref_id`.

**Elision.** Any block longer than 24000 characters is truncated by this file with an explicit `[...N chars omitted, see <ref>]` marker. The elision ledger is at the end of this file.

**Truncation that is not ours.** Markers of the form `... [TRUNCATED: N lines omitted] ...` appear inside some quoted outputs. Those are the harness's own truncation, present in the source bytes, and are reproduced verbatim — they are not an elision by this file. They occur 12 times below.

**Generation.** Written programmatically; no block was typed by hand. Self-verification report at the end of this file.

**Scope of this file.** Verdict blocks; every distinct failure signature at first occurrence with full [A]/[B]/[C]/[D] quads; the test phase in its entirety; the forced-floor / `TEST_ATTEMPT_REQUIRED` events; the final test gate. **No judgment section** — protocol §4 reserves judgment for the aggregate.

## 3. `TEST_ATTEMPT_REQUIRED` and the forced-floor event — quoted whole

`control_events.jsonl` carries exactly one `forced_action` event in this run: sequence 256, `intent_source: "controller"`, `policy: "test_attempt_required"`. The chain that produced it and the chain it produced are quoted whole below, in file order. Occurrences of the string `TEST_ATTEMPT_REQUIRED` in `control_events.jsonl`: sequences 252, 254, 255.

### 3.11 The controller-owned action in the loop record and the next branch entry

`contexts/phase_test.json` has no `history` entry for the forced `project(action=analyze)`: its `history[5]` is the refused claim and its `history[6]` is the next model action. The forced action appears only in `control_events.jsonl` at sequences 256, 258, 259; its `loop_decision` carries `evidence_ref: ""`. Both quoted:
